# Graph Report - Quant-Nanggroe-AI-worktree  (2026-07-12)

## Corpus Check
- 2472 files · ~2,322,471 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 52428 nodes · 86204 edges · 2900 communities (2119 shown, 781 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 83% EXTRACTED · 17% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 14996 edges (avg confidence: 0.54)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `d4773d17`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
